### [105] [Long-range spin-polarized Josephson effect in ballistic S/F/S junctions with precessing magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14390)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,M. Mansouri,M. Breitkreiz,P. W. Brouwer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了具有均匀进动磁化的弹道N/F/S和S/F/S结的理论，进动磁化产生长程等自旋超导关联，导致非平衡安德烈夫束缚态分布和强非正弦电流-相位关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁化进动对超导-铁磁异质结中输运性质的影响，特别是进动磁化如何产生长程等自旋超导关联，以及这种非平衡态如何影响安德烈夫束缚态和约瑟夫森效应。

Method: 发展弹道N/F/S和S/F/S结的理论模型，考虑均匀进动磁化，分析非平衡安德烈夫束缚态分布，推导电流-相位关系，并分别处理部分极化和完全极化（半金属极限）的铁磁体情况。

Result: 对于大进动角，电流-相位关系呈现强非正弦特性；在半金属极限下，磁化进动可将结从亚能隙电流为零的"关"状态切换到具有有限安德烈夫电导和有限约瑟夫森电流的"开"状态。

Conclusion: 磁化进动在超导-铁磁异质结中产生长程等自旋超导关联，导致非平衡安德烈夫束缚态分布，显著改变输运特性，特别是在半金属中可实现通过磁化进动控制的开关效应。

Abstract: We present a theory of ballistic N/F/S and S/F/S junctions with a uniformly precessing magnetization, which generates long-range equal-spin superconducting correlations [Takahashi et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 99, 057003 (2007), Houzet, Phys. Rev. Lett. 101, 057009 (2008)]. The non-equilibrium distribution of Andreev bound states leads to a strongly non-sinusoidal current-phase relationship for large precession angles. We derive detailed results for ballistic junctions involving partially and fully polarized ferromagnets. In the fully polarized half-metal limit, the magnetization precession switches the junction from an "off" state with vanishing subgap current to an "on" state with finite Andreev conductance and finite Josephson current.

</details>


### [106] [Josephson phase shift and diode effect due to the inverse spin Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14521)
*Gen Tatara,Yositake Takane,Aurelien Manchon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究超导体-正常金属-超导体结中由空间反演不变的spin-orbit相互作用引起的直接和逆自旋霍尔效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在保持空间反演对称性的系统中实现自旋霍尔效应的机制，不同于需要破坏结构反演对称性的Rashba系统

Method: 理论分析超导体-正常金属-超导体结，考虑空间反演不变的自旋轨道相互作用，研究超电流诱导的自旋霍尔效应和静态磁场诱导的逆效应

Result: 超电流诱导静态自旋积累（两端极化相反）；静态磁场诱导异常相移，结合高次谐波产生二极管效应；机制不要求破坏结构反演对称性

Conclusion: 在保持空间反演对称性的系统中可实现自旋霍尔效应和二极管效应，为自旋电子学应用提供了新途径

Abstract: We theoretically study the direct and inverse spin Hall effects in a superconductor-normal metal-superconductor junction induced by a spin-orbit interaction that is invariant under spatial inversion. We show that a supercurrent induces a spin Hall effect, leading to a static spin accumulation with opposite polarizations at the two edges, analogous to that in normal conductors. For the inverse effect, we consider a spatially inhomogeneous static magnetic field and show that it induces an anomalous phase shift, which, in the presence of higher harmonics, results in a diode effect. Unlike Rashba systems, the present mechanism does not require broken structural inversion symmetry.

</details>


### [107] [Effect of Rashba spin-orbit coupling on Faraday rotation in an extended Haldane model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14665)
*Yuan Fang,Yixiang Wang,Xiaopu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究Rashba自旋轨道耦合对扩展Haldane模型中法拉第旋转光谱的影响，发现Rashba SOC可调控FR峰值位置和强度，为磁光器件设计提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑材料的法拉第旋转特性为新型磁光器件提供了前景，但Rashba自旋轨道耦合对FR光谱的影响尚未系统研究，需要探索通过SOC工程优化器件性能的可能性。

Method: 在扩展Haldane模型中引入Rashba SOC和交换劈裂，采用Kubo公式计算FR光谱，通过低能有效哈密顿量展开到二次项进行验证，并与紧束缚模型计算结果对比。

Result: 在陈数C=2区域，无交换劈裂时FR角可超过4°，峰值位置可通过Rashba SOC调控；加入交换劈裂后，在宽频率范围内出现近乎平坦的FR谱线，FR峰值随Rashba SOC强度单调增加。

Conclusion: Rashba SOC通过开启额外跃迁通道增强FR峰值，其工程化可用于设计和优化磁光器件特性，为拓扑材料在磁光应用中的性能调控提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Utilization of Faraday rotation (FR) properties of topological materials offers a promising route toward novel magneto-optical devices. We systematically investigated the effect of Rashba spin-orbit coupling (SOC) on FR spectra in an extended Haldane model, which incorporates Rashba SOC and exchange splitting into the original spinless Haldane framework. Using the Kubo formalism, we calculated the FR spectra across the model's rich topological phase diagram. We found that in the Chern number C=2 region, in the absence of exchange splitting, the FR angle can exceed 4$^\circ$ and its peak position is tunable by the Rashba SOC. In contrast, with the inclusion of exchange splitting, a nearly flat FR profile emerges over a broad frequency range, and the FR peak values increase monotonically with the Rashba SOC strength. The Rashba SOC opens additional transition channels, whose net contribution constructively enhances the FR peak. Furthermore, we derived a low-energy effective Hamiltonian expanded up to quadratic terms, the results of which are in good agreement with tight-binding model calculations, thereby validating our numerical results. Our findings suggest that magneto-optical device characteristics can be designed and optimized through Rashba SOC engineering.

</details>


### [108] [Orbitals of Artificial Atoms in a Gapped Two-Dimensional Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14737)
*Mong-Wen Gu,Aizhan Sabitova,Taner Esat,Christian Wagner,F. Stefan Tautz,Aleksandr Rodin,Ruslan Temirov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 人工原子在二维分子薄膜中的轨道态可视化，揭示了类似真实原子的s和p轨道，以及由带隙真空产生的新型准一维局域态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人工原子如何相互作用和成键对于设计具有定制电子特性的新材料至关重要，需要探索人工原子在二维带隙环境中的轨道行为

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜可视化在具有抛物线能带和多个部分能隙的二维分子薄膜中图案化纳米结构的束缚态

Result: 观察到最低能态从能带底部分离，类似于自然原子的s和p轨道，并以相同方式成键；同时发现了由带隙二维真空产生的新型准一维局域态，这些态在真实原子中没有对应物

Conclusion: 人工原子不仅模拟真实原子的轨道行为，还能产生全新的轨道类型，这些由周围电子真空主导的准一维局域态丰富了化学的轨道词汇，为材料设计提供了新途径

Abstract: Advances in nanotechnology now allow the creation of artificial atoms - engineered structures whose electronic states closely mimic those of real atoms. Understanding how these artificial atoms interact and bond is key to designing new materials with tailored electronic properties. Here, we use scanning tunnelling microscopy to visualise the bound states of nanostructures patterned in a two-dimensional molecular film featuring a parabolic band with multiple partial energy gaps. The lowest-energy states split off from the bottom of the band and resemble the familiar $s$ and $p$ orbitals of natural atoms, even bonding in the same way. Yet, artificial atoms go beyond this analogy: the gapped two-dimensional vacuum in which they reside gives rise to entirely new orbitals with no counterparts in real atoms. These quasi-one-dimensional localised states enrich the orbital vocabulary of chemistry, adding a new class of orbitals that are predominantly shaped by the surrounding electronic vacuum.

</details>


### [109] [Thermal conductivity tuning of scalable nanopatterned silicon membranes measured with a three-probe method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14770)
*Jose M. Sojo-Gordillo,Alex Rodriguez-Iglesias,Dominik M. Koch,Arianna Nigro,Iñigo Martin-Fernandez,Marta Fernandez-Regulez,Marc Salleras,Ilaria Zardo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种通过纳米孔图案化硅膜实现热导率可控降低的方法，结合了嵌段共聚物自组装纳米孔制备技术和改进的三探针热导率测量技术，实现了室温下热导率5倍的降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子硅结构作为可集成和可扩展的纳米系统在调控热输运方面具有潜力，但其复杂制造工艺限制了广泛应用。同时，悬浮纳米结构薄膜的热性能可靠表征面临挑战，热接触电阻常影响测量精度。

Method: 采用嵌段共聚物自组装方法制备纳米孔硅膜（周期63nm，孔径35nm），并引入改进的三探针技术，实现复杂薄膜系统中稳健、定量、空间分辨的热导率测量，同时考虑热接触伪影。

Result: 方法在30-350K温度范围内验证，未图案化40nm厚硅薄膜室温热导率为46.5W/m·K。纳米孔蚀刻深度控制成为调控热输运的有效参数，完全蚀穿膜在室温下热导率降低5倍至7.3W/m·K。

Conclusion: 纳米孔蚀刻深度控制是声子硅中调控热输运的有效参数，嵌段共聚物自组装结合改进三探针测量技术为复杂纳米结构薄膜的热性能研究和调控提供了可靠途径。

Abstract: Phononic silicon structures have emerged as an integrable and scalable nanosystem for tailoring thermal transport. However, their widespread adoption has been limited by their complex fabrication pathways. Alongside, the reliable characterization of thermal properties in suspended nanostructured films remains challenging, as thermal contact resistances often hinder the accuracy of measurements.
  In this work, we demonstrate a clear and controllable reduction of thermal conductivity in nanopatterned silicon membranes. A block copolymer self-assembly approach is employed to fabricate nanoholed silicon films with a pitch of 63 nm and hole diameters of 35 nm. Additionally, we introduce an extension of the three-probe technique that enables robust, quantitative, and spatially resolved thermal conductivity measurements in complex thin-film systems, accounting for thermal contact artifacts.
  The method is validated through measurements on unpatterned 40 nm-thick silicon thin films between 30 and 350 K, yielding a room-temperature thermal conductivity of 46.5 W/m.K. Finally, we further show that controlled etching of the nanoholes provides a powerful means to tune thermal transport in the overall studied temperature range, establishing hole etch depth control as an effective parameter in phononic silicon. Specifically, a fivefold reduction in thermal conductivity is achieved, reaching 7.3 W/m.K for fully etched-through membranes at room temperature.

</details>


### [110] [Formalizing Poisson-Boltzmann Theory for Field-Tunable Nanofluidic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14777)
*Zhongyuan Zhao,Chudi Qi,Yuheng Li,Shoushan Fan,Qunqing Li,Yang Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文建立了纳米流体离子输运的正式理论框架，基于泊松-玻尔兹曼理论重新表述，揭示了不同EDL区域，并预测了静电调制的两个基本热力学极限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米流体器件中非常规离子输运对能源和信息技术具有重要意义，但实验研究快速发展而理论解释尚未达到形式化和统一化水平

Method: 正式重新表述泊松-玻尔兹曼理论，在参数空间上识别不同的EDL区域，基于区域分类建立可调纳米流体输运的形式化框架

Result: 框架能够重现观察到的电导率-浓度标度行为，合理化具有可重构极性的离子晶体管，并预测静电调制的两个基本热力学极限（60 mV/dec和120 mV/dec）

Conclusion: 该框架准确、可推广且可扩展，能够解释受限空间中广泛的离子输运现象，为纳米流体器件的理性设计提供了理论基础

Abstract: Nanofluidic devices prompts unconventional ion transports appealing to energy and information technologies, thanks to the susceptibility of confined electric double layers (EDL) to various external physical fields. Although experimental studies advance rapidly, the rationalization of field-tunable nanofluidic transports has not reached a formalized and unified level. Here we formally reformulate the Poisson-Boltzmann theory and reveal distinct EDL regimes on the parameter space. Based on the regime classification, we establish a formal framework for the tunable nanofluidic transport, which reproduces the observed conductivity-concentration scaling behaviors, rationalizes the ionic transistors with reconfigurable polarities, and predicts two fundamental thermodynamic limits for electrostatic modulation (60 mV/dec and 120 mV/dec). Being accurate, generalizable and extensible, this framework can account for a wide range of ion transports in confined spaces.

</details>


### [111] [Weak Magnetic Sensing via Floquet Driving in an Active Cavity Magnon Coupled System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14780)
*Fan Yang,Xudong Wang,Lijun Yan,Yue Zhao,Jinwei Rao,Lihui Bai,Shishen Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于PCB实现的微波腔-YIG耦合系统，通过电调增益补偿损耗，利用Floquet调制检测交变弱磁场，室温工作，灵敏度达121 pT/√Hz


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高灵敏度弱磁场检测技术通常需要低温操作或庞大设备，限制了实际应用。需要开发能在室温工作、小型化的高灵敏度磁场传感器。

Method: 在PCB上实现有源微波腔与钇铁石榴石(YIG)的耦合系统。通过引入电调增益补偿腔体损耗，提高品质因子和信号强度。在交变磁场作用下产生Floquet调制，通过驱动特定混合模式并测量产生的Floquet边带来检测弱磁场。

Result: 开发出小型化室温工作磁场传感器，检测极限达到121 pT/√Hz，实现了高灵敏度弱磁场检测。

Conclusion: 基于PCB的微波腔-YIG耦合系统结合电调增益补偿，为室温、小型化高灵敏度弱磁场检测提供了有效解决方案，在121 pT/√Hz的灵敏度下实现了实用化的弱磁场传感。

Abstract: While significant advancements have been made in weak magnetic field detection, conventional high-sensitivity techniques are often limited by requirements for cryogenic operation or bulky setups. In this work, we develop a sensitive alternating magnetic field sensor based on a coupled system of an active microwave cavity and yttrium iron garnet (YIG), with the components implemented on printed circuit boards (PCBs). By introducing electrically tunable gain to compensate for cavity losses, we substantially improve both the quality factor and the signal intensity. Under the coupled system, Floquet modulation is induced by the alternating magnetic field, allowing for weak field detection by driving a specific hybrid mode and measuring the resulting Floquet sidebands. This miniaturized device operates at room temperature, achieving a detection limit of 121 pT/\sqrt{Hz}.

</details>


### [112] [Layer-dependent quantum transport in KV2Se2O-based altermagnetic tunnel junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14817)
*Yue Zhao,Bin Xiao,Jiawei Liu,Hui Zeng,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于KV₂Se₂O/SrTiO₃/KV₂Se₂O设计的交变磁隧道结(AMTJ)展现出层数依赖的量子输运特性，奇偶层数界面构型差异导致传输振荡行为，预测可实现4.6×10⁷%的巨隧道磁阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁隧道结(MTJ)的持续微型化受到杂散磁场的限制。交变磁体结合了铁磁体和反铁磁体的优势，提供无杂散场、高本征频率等特性，为开发高密度自旋电子器件提供新途径。

Method: 基于最近报道的交变金属候选材料KV₂Se₂O，设计KV₂Se₂O/SrTiO₃/KV₂Se₂O交变磁隧道结结构。采用密度泛函理论结合非平衡格林函数方法，研究层数依赖的量子输运特性和隧道磁阻。

Result: AMTJ器件的传输表现出明显的振荡行为，依赖于SrTiO₃半导体的层数奇偶性。奇层器件中O-Se界面具有平滑有效势，支持横向动量(k||)输运通道，传输增强；偶层器件中Ti-Se界面势垒更陡，阻碍量子输运。使用4层SrTiO₃可实现4.6×10⁷%的巨隧道磁阻。

Conclusion: 研究不仅提供了AMTJ中量子输运的物理理解，还揭示了势垒界面工程是调控磁电性能的有效策略，为开发高性能自旋电子器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic tunnel junction (MTJ) is the key component to enable information access and increasing number of MTJs is integrated to develop high-density spintronic devices. However, continuous miniaturization of the conventional MTJs is hindered by stray magnetic fields. Altermagnets, combining the advantages of both ferromagnets and antiferromagnets, provide a promising alternative to fabricate versatile MTJs with exotic properties, such as giant spin splitting, high intrinsic frequency, and absence of stray fields. Inspired by the altermagnetic metal candidate KV2Se2O reported recently, we design an altermagnetic tunnel junction (AMTJ) based on KV2Se2O/SrTiO3/KV2Se2O. Using density functional theory combined with non-equilibrium Green's function, we investigate the layer-dependent quantum transport properties and the tunneling magnetoresistance (TMR) of such AMTJ device. Our calculated results reveal that the transmission of the AMTJ device exhibits a pronounced oscillation behavior dependent on the number of layers of the SrTiO3 semiconductor, which is attributed to the interface configuration determined by parity of the layer number. In odd-layer devices, the electron-rich O-Se interface exhibits a smooth effective potential and enables transverse momentum (k||) transport channels, leading to enhanced transmission. In contrast, in even-layer devices, the Ti-Se interface presents a steeper effective potential, impeding quantum transport through transverse momentum (k||) channels. A giant TMR of 4.6*10^7% is predicted to be realized by using a 4-layer SrTiO3. Our findings not only provide physical understanding relevant to the quantum transport in AMTJs, but also unveil that the barrier interface engineering is a strategy to tune the magnetoelectric performance.

</details>


### [113] [Poor man's Majorana bound states in quantum dot based Kitaev chain coupled to a photonic cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15036)
*Francesco Buonemani,Alvaro Gómez-León,Marco Schirò,Olesia Dmytruk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 量子点平台实现Kitaev链哈密顿量，通过光子腔调控参数达到甜点条件，实现poor man's Majorana束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点平台为实现Kitaev链哈密顿量和Majorana束缚态提供了有前景的途径，但需要精细调节参数达到甜点条件。本文研究光子腔如何影响量子点系统中的配对项，并探索通过腔光子态来调控粒子相互作用，实现poor man's Majorana束缚态。

Method: 基于先前工作[Phys. Rev. B 111, 155410 (2025)]，考虑量子点与光子腔耦合的微观模型。分析光子耦合对有效哈密顿量的影响，特别是对配对项的修正。研究不同光子态（零光子态、单光子态、多光子态）对粒子相互作用的调控作用。

Result: 光子耦合在微观模型中产生有效哈密顿量，腔影响配对项。但即使在这种情况下，仍可通过光子态调控实现甜点条件：零光子态可抵消吸引相互作用，单光子态可屏蔽排斥相互作用。多光子态下跳跃振幅被抑制，导致简并谱。

Conclusion: 量子光可用于工程化poor man's Majorana束缚态，通过腔嵌入实现参数调控。不同光子态可有效调控粒子相互作用，为实现Majorana束缚态提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum dot based platforms offer a promising route towards realizing the Kitaev chain Hamiltonian hosting Majorana bound states (MBSs). Poor man's MBSs arise in a two-site Kitaev chain when the parameters of the system are fine-tuned to the sweet spot. Based on our previous work [Phys. Rev. B 111, 155410 (2025)], we consider a microscopic model for the Kitaev chain based on quantum dots with proximity effect embedded in a photonic cavity. We find that the photon coupling in the microscopic model yields an effective Hamiltonian where the cavity affects the pairing term. However, we demonstrate that even in this case, it is possible to screen particle interactions and reach the sweet spot condition for the emergence of the poor man's MBSs. In particular, we find that attractive particle interactions can be canceled for the cavity prepared in the zero-photon state, while repulsive ones can be screened with a cavity prepared in the one-photon state. Furthermore, in case of a large number of photons in the cavity, we find that the hopping amplitudes are suppressed resulting in a degenerate spectrum. This motivates the use of quantum light for engineering poor man's MBSs with cavity embedding.

</details>


### [114] [New frontiers in quantum science and technology using van der Waals Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15276)
*Joydip Sarkar,Ayshi Mukherjee,Amit Basu,Ritajit Kundu,Arijit Kundu,Mandar M. Deshmukh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该综述论文探讨了基于范德华材料的约瑟夫森结器件的最新进展，这些器件利用二维材料的独特性质实现了传统约瑟夫森结无法达到的新功能，为超导量子技术开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森结在制造工艺上已达到工业水平，但受限于材料特性，难以探索新的物理现象和功能。范德华材料库的多样性及其独特的二维特性为约瑟夫森器件提供了全新的设计自由度，能够实现传统器件无法达到的对称性控制和量子态调控。

Method: 通过综述分析范德华约瑟夫森结的研究进展，结合先进的制造技术，整合具有不同性质的范德华材料，探索新型器件结构和物理机制。特别关注材料对称性控制、二维系统特有的哈密顿量实现，以及利用二维异质结构中长弛豫时间的激发态。

Result: 范德华约瑟夫森结展现出传统器件无法实现的新功能，包括独特的对称性控制、新型量子态的实现，以及作为高效量子传感器传输通道的潜力。这些进展为超导量子技术带来了革命性变化，特别是在量子计算和超灵敏混合传感器应用方面。

Conclusion: 范德华约瑟夫森结代表了超导电子学领域的范式转变，通过结合扭曲电子学和拓扑学，有望重新定义超导量子技术。虽然面临可扩展性的挑战，但这一领域的发展潜力巨大，为量子计算和传感器应用提供了新的技术路线图。

Abstract: Over the last decade, the development of Josephson devices based on van der Waals (vdW) materials has advanced rapidly, representing a paradigm shift driven by the advent of 2D materials. The diverse vdW materials library, combined with advanced fabrication techniques, enables the integration of materials with vastly disparate properties for scientific exploration. The vdW Josephson junctions (JJs) offer a unique route to explore novel functionalities and associated physics that remain inaccessible in conventional JJs, which have reached an industrial level in terms of fabrication. Beyond material diversity, vdW crystalline materials offer fundamental new control over device symmetries, enabling the realization of Hamiltonians unique to 2D systems. Furthermore, the long relaxation times of myriad excitations in 2D heterostructures open possibilities for creating exquisite quantum sensors, with the 2D material itself acting as an efficient bus for transmitting excitations to the active sensing element. This creative explosion in vdW-based superconducting electronics is rapidly growing, and our review highlights the resulting devices and physics. The confluence of vdW JJs with twistronics and topology has the potential to redefine superconducting quantum technology, enabling applications from quantum computation to ultra-sensitive hybrid sensors. While opportunities abound with vdW JJs, the challenge of scalability must be surmounted for translation into real-world devices. This review synthesizes current developments and offers a roadmap for researchers navigating this burgeoning field.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [$μ$SR study of time-reversal symmetry constraints and bulk superfluid response in Li$_{0.95}$FeAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14376)
*Rustem Khasanov,Hubertus Luetkens,Nikolai D. Zhigadlo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过μSR测量证实Li₀.₉₅FeAs是体多能隙超导体，无时间反演对称性破缺，超流密度主要来自中等和小能隙的费米面


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Li₀.₉₅FeAs超导体的超导性质，特别是时间反演对称性是否破缺、超导是否为体性质、能隙结构特征，以及μSR与ARPES等不同探测手段的测量结果如何协调

Method: 采用零场和横向场μ子自旋旋转/弛豫（μSR）技术，通过高压自熔剂法生长的Li₀.₉₅FeAs单晶样品，测量电子弛豫率、涡旋响应、穿透深度和超流密度

Result: 零场μSR显示超导转变时电子弛豫率无变化，表明无时间反演对称性破缺；横向场μSR证实强磁通钉扎和可忽略的非超导贡献，超导为体性质；测得面内磁穿透深度λ_ab=245(15)nm；超流密度温度依赖可用有效双能隙模型描述，Δ₁=2.0(2)meV，Δ₂=0.7(2)meV；μSR响应主要来自携带中等和小能隙的费米面，最大能隙带仅贡献约3%超流密度

Conclusion: Li₀.₉₅FeAs是体多能隙超导体，无时间反演对称性破缺；μSR测量结果与ARPES等表面敏感探测手段的能隙尺度差异源于不同费米面对超流密度的贡献权重不同，最大能隙带对μSR信号贡献极小

Abstract: We report zero-field (ZF) and transverse-field (TF) muon-spin rotation/relaxation ($μ$SR) measurements on superconducting Li$_{0.95}$FeAs ($T_{\rm c}\simeq16.0$ K) grown by a high-pressure self-flux method. The ZF-$μ$SR data show no detectable change of the electronic relaxation rate on cooling through $T_{\rm c}$, providing no evidence for time-reversal-symmetry breaking in the superconducting state. TF-$μ$SR measurements reveal a well-developed vortex response with strong flux pinning and a negligible nonsuperconducting contribution, confirming that superconductivity is a bulk property of the sample. From the second moment of the internal field distribution we determine a low-temperature in-plane magnetic penetration depth $λ_{ab}= 245(15)$ nm. The temperature dependence of the normalized superfluid density is well described by an effective two-gap model with $Δ_1 = 2.0(2)$ meV and $Δ_2 = 0.7(2)$ meV. A quantitative comparison with ARPES-based band weights shows that the $μ$SR response is dominated by the Fermi-surface sheets carrying the intermediate and small superconducting gaps, whereas the band hosting the largest gap contributes only about 3\% to the total superfluid density and is therefore not resolved in the present analysis. Taken together, these results establish Li$_{0.95}$FeAs as a bulk multigap superconductor without detectable time-reversal symmetry breaking and show how $μ$SR reconciles the gap scales reported by bulk and surface-sensitive probes in this multiband system.

</details>


### [116] [Revisiting apparent ideal diamagnetism at ambient conditions in graphene-n-heptane-permalloy systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14395)
*Rajendra Dulal,Serafim Teknowijoyo,Sara Chahid,Vahan Nikoghosyan,Armen Gulian*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究发现先前报道的石墨烯-正庚烷-坡莫合金系统中的理想抗磁性现象实际上是由坡莫合金箔不均匀性和实验几何结构引起的磁场重新分布效应，石墨烯并非关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清先前报道的石墨烯-正庚烷-坡莫合金系统中观察到的理想抗磁性现象的真实物理机制，因为实验中出现了不一致的行为（信号冻结和偶尔的顺磁响应）。

Method: 通过对比实验方法：1）在原始石墨烯-正庚烷-坡莫合金系统中进行测量；2）在没有石墨烯的情况下进行相同测量；3）分析坡莫合金箔的不均匀性和实验几何结构对磁场分布的影响。

Result: 在没有石墨烯的实验中观察到了类似的信号，表明石墨烯不是观测效应的原因。研究发现坡莫合金箔的不均匀性和实验几何结构引起的磁场重新分布可以在亚毫高斯测量中模拟理想抗磁性。

Conclusion: 先前结果的解释需要修正：观测到的"理想抗磁性"实际上是磁场重新分布的伪影，而非石墨烯的特殊性质。研究强调了在超低磁场测量中解释结果时需要格外谨慎。

Abstract: We previously reported apparent ideal diamagnetism at ambient conditions in a graphene-n-heptane-permalloy system. At the same time, the experiments revealed inconsistent behavior, including signal freezing and occasional paramagnetic responses. Further measurements performed without graphene produced similar signals, indicating that graphene is not responsible for the observed effects. The results suggest that magnetic field redistribution caused by inhomogeneities in the permalloy foil and experimental geometry can mimic ideal diamagnetism in sub-milligauss measurements. These findings revise the interpretation of our earlier results and emphasize caution in interpreting ultra-low-field magnetic measurements.

</details>


### [117] [Direct laser micromachining of superconducting terahertz Josephson plasma emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14485)
*Reo Yamaguchi,Takuma Sakurai,Kazuhiro Yamaki,Akinobu Irie,Junichiro Kato,Taichiro Nishio,Shigeyuki Ishida,Hiroshi Eisaki,Manabu Tsujimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用紫外激光直接微加工Bi-2212单晶快速制备太赫兹约瑟夫森等离子体发射器，无需掩模，保持约瑟夫森结结构完整，实现稳定太赫兹辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种快速、无需掩模的制备方法，用于制造太赫兹约瑟夫森等离子体发射器，以克服传统制造工艺的复杂性和成本问题，同时探索低成本电极材料替代方案。

Method: 采用直接紫外激光微加工技术处理Bi-2212单晶，通过激光烧蚀形成器件结构，同时保持晶体内部约瑟夫森结的完整性。使用Ag、Cu和Cr三种电极材料进行对比实验，通过光谱和偏振分析表征辐射特性。

Result: 所有电极材料均能产生太赫兹辐射，Cu电极性能与Ag相当，提供低成本替代方案。辐射为椭圆偏振，主要由几何腔共振模式主导。加工宽度和深度受Bi-2212各向异性热导率控制，而非光学光斑尺寸限制。

Conclusion: 直接激光微加工方法为约瑟夫森等离子体发射器提供了一种快速、通用的制备技术，适用于超导电子学和太赫兹器件领域，具有工艺简单、成本低的优势。

Abstract: We demonstrate a rapid, maskless fabrication method for superconducting terahertz Josephson plasma emitters (JPEs) based on direct ultraviolet laser micromachining of Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$ (Bi-2212) single crystals. Although machining debris is formed near the processed regions, uniform stacks of intrinsic Josephson junctions are preserved inside the crystal, enabling stable terahertz emission. Devices fabricated with Ag, Cu, and Cr electrodes all exhibited terahertz radiation, with Cu electrodes showing performance comparable to Ag while offering a low-cost alternative. Spectroscopic and polarization analyses indicate that the emitted radiation is elliptically polarized and dominated by the geometrical cavity resonance mode. Structural and electrical characterizations reveal that the machining width and depth are not limited by the optical spot size but are governed by the anisotropic thermal conductivity of Bi-2212, consistent with a thermally dominated laser ablation process. This direct laser micromachining approach provides a fast and versatile fabrication technique for JPEs and is broadly applicable to superconducting electronics and terahertz devices.

</details>


### [118] [Wide-field magnetic imaging of shielding-current-driven vortex rearrangement under local heating using diamond quantum sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14578)
*Ryoei Ota,Shunsuke Nishimura,Koki Honda,Takeyuki Tsuji,Taro Yamashita,Takayuki Iwasaki,Mutsuko Hatano,Kento Sasaki,Kensuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用金刚石NV系综宽场磁成像技术，实时观测了NbN薄膜中涡旋在激光加热和磁场变化下的重排过程，揭示了涡旋动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和控制超导体中的涡旋运动对于抑制超导器件中的耗散以及开发基于涡旋的器件应用至关重要。需要定量研究涡旋的实时动态行为。

Method: 使用完美对准的金刚石NV系综进行宽场磁成像，定量测量NbN薄膜中涡旋的杂散磁场分布。在局部激光加热下逐步改变外加磁场，连续测量超过100分钟，捕捉涡旋构型的实时重排。

Result: 观测到涡旋构型在实空间和实时中的重排，这与局部激光加热导致的钉扎力减弱以及磁场变化诱导的屏蔽电流产生的洛伦兹力作用一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了对涡旋动力学的深入理解，并提出了潜在应用，包括将涡旋排除在超导器件的敏感区域之外以及在基于涡旋的器件中进行涡旋定位。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling vortex motion in superconductors are important both for suppressing dissipation in superconducting devices and for device applications that exploit vortices. In this work, we quantitatively imaged the stray magnetic field distribution of vortices in an NbN thin film by wide-field magnetic imaging using a perfectly aligned diamond NV ensemble. By continuously measuring while stepwise varying the applied magnetic field under local laser heating, we captured a rearrangement of the vortex configuration in real space and in real time over more than 100 min. The observed vortex rearrangement is consistent with a reduction of the pinning force due to local laser heating and with the Lorentz force exerted by shielding currents induced by the field variation. These results provide insight into vortex dynamics and suggest potential applications, including vortex exclusion from sensitive regions of superconducting devices and vortex positioning in vortex-based devices.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Landscape of Superconducting Diodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14623)
*Muhammad Nadeem,Xiaolin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究将超导二极管(SDs)的量子特性映射到量子技术架构中，解决当前量子硬件在控制和可扩展性方面的挑战，通过SDs的内置非线性、非互易性和量子功能实现片上集成c-QED，促进温度匹配的经典-量子混合工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子技术硬件面临两大基本挑战：1) 非线性非互易电路元件（量子处理器关键组件）通常复杂、庞大且耗散；2) 经典控制电子学（~K温度）与量子处理器（~mK温度）之间的温度梯度导致可扩展性困难。需要寻找集成解决方案来克服这些限制。

Method: 将超导二极管的量子特性映射到量子技术架构中，利用SDs的内置非线性、非互易性和量子功能，实现片上集成电路量子电动力学(c-QED)。通过热力学约束和场效应的调控，构建量子增强的SD平台。

Result: SDs能够实现噪声弹性量子比特和量子比特接口的可扩展集成，支持高效功率传输、相干控制和存储、高保真度读取以及量子极限放大。同时实现经典与量子工作流的热兼容性、等温全电控制和片上可扩展性。

Conclusion: 超导二极管为超导电路量子硬件提供了关键解决方案，通过温度匹配的经典、量子和混合工作流，有望在量子技术架构中发挥关键作用，推动可扩展量子处理器的实现。

Abstract: This study maps the quantum landscape of superconducting diodes (SDs) \cite{nadeem23} onto the quantum technology architecture, which is currently constrained by fundamental challenges in control and scalability. In the existing non-integrated quantum technology hardware, control and scalability related issues emerge at two fronts: First, nonlinear and nonreciprocal circuit elements, which are essential building blocks for quantum processors, are often complex, bulky, and dissipative. Second, the temperature gradient between classical control electronics ($T_C\gtrsim$ K), which is also dissipative, and the quantum processor at cryogenic temperatures ($T_Q\sim$ mK) makes scalability even more challenging. The main focus is to reveal how the built-in nonlinearity, nonreciprocity, and quantum functionalities of SDs are significant for on-chip integrated circuit quantum electrodynamics (c-QED), enabling scalable integration of noise-resilient qubit and qubit-interfaces for efficient power delivery, coherent control and memory, high-fidelity readout, and quantum-limited amplification. To this end, this study will also shed light on how thermodynamic constraints and field effects can be harnessed within a quantum-enhanced SD platform, thereby enabling thermal compatibility between classical and quantum workflows, isothermal all-electrical control, and on-chip scalability. This perspective is expected to play a pivotal role in the advancement of superconducting circuit-based quantum hardware with temperature-matched classical, quantum, and hybrid workflows.

</details>


### [120] [Interlayer hybridization enables superconductivity in bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14701)
*Shilong Zhang,Meng Zhang,Qilin Luo,Zihao Tao,Hsiao-Yu Huang,Kunhao Li,Jie Li,Junchi Fu,Di-Jing Huang,Yanwu Xie,Yi Lu,Yingying Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过稳定超导(La,Pr)₃Ni₂O₇薄膜并利用X射线光谱技术，揭示了双层镍酸盐超导的微观机制：面内d_{x²-y²}态形成巡游骨架，而超导性仅当d_{z²}-p_z-d_{z²}层间杂化发展时出现，伴随静态自旋序抑制和自旋激发强烈阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐为超越铜酸盐和铁基超导体的高温超导提供了新途径，但双层镍酸盐中超导性所需的电子重组机制尚未解决，主要由于难以直接探测超导相。

Method: 通过保护性覆盖层稳定超导(La,Pr)₃Ni₂O₇薄膜，利用X射线吸收谱(XAS)和共振非弹性X射线散射(RIXS)直接光谱探测，结合理论分析，研究绝缘、超导和金属相中的面内和面外电子态、自旋和轨道激发以及自旋密度波演化。

Result: 发现面内d_{x²-y²}态形成巡游骨架，超导性仅在相干d_{z²}-p_z-d_{z²}层间杂化发展时出现，伴随静态自旋序抑制和自旋激发强烈阻尼。氧化学计量和外延应变都作用于该层间通道，使超导性处于层间相干性和关联强度的狭窄窗口内。

Conclusion: 这些发现确定了双层镍酸盐超导所需的微观要素，并提供了其出现的多轨道图像，为理解镍酸盐超导机制提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates offer a new route to high-temperature superconductivity beyond the cuprates and iron-pnictides. However, the electronic reorganization that enables superconductivity in bilayer nickelates remain unresolved, largely due to the difficulty of directly probing the superconducting phase. Here, we overcome this limitation by stabilizing superconducting (La,Pr)$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films with a protective capping layer, thereby enabling direct spectroscopic access via X-ray absorption and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering. We resolve the evolution of in-plane and out-of-plane electronic states, spin and orbital excitations, and spin-density-waves across insulating, superconducting, and metallic regimes. Combining experimental results with theoretical analysis, we show that the in-plane $d_{x^2-y^2}$ states form an itinerant backbone, while superconductivity emerges only when coherent $d_{z^2}$-$p_z$-$d_{z^2}$ interlayer hybridization develops, accompanied by suppressed static spin order and strongly damped spin excitations. Oxygen stoichiometry and epitaxial strain both act on this interlayer channel, placing superconductivity within a narrow window of interlayer coherence and correlation strength. These findings identify the microscopic ingredients required for superconductivity in bilayer nickelates and provide a multiorbital picture of its emergence.

</details>


### [121] [Fermi-liquid versus non-Fermi-liquid/'strange-metal' fits to the electrical resistivity in the quantum critical magnetic regime of an unconventional superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14952)
*W. Knafo,T. Thebault,K. Somesh,G. Lapertot,G. Knebel,D. Braithwaite,D. Aoki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在UTe₂重费米子超导体中，通过分析不同质量样品的电阻率数据，发现接近超导相的非费米液体行为可能掩盖了低温下"隐藏"的费米液体T²行为，表明需要高质量样品来确认量子临界行为是否真实存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子临界点是否存在于超导相内部对于理解非常规超导机制至关重要。在UTe₂中，非常规超导发生在磁场诱导的变磁性量子相变附近，需要实验验证非费米液体行为是否真正源于本征的零温量子临界区域。

Method: 对两个不同质量的UTe₂样品，在磁场与b轴夹角θ≈35-40°的条件下测量电阻率。分别用费米液体函数ρ=ρ₀+AT²和非费米液体函数ρ=ρ₀+AₙTⁿ拟合数据，分析拟合参数特别是剩余电阻率ρ₀的物理合理性。

Result: 在接近40T以上诱导的超导相附近，从Tⁿ拟合中提取出非物理的剩余电阻率ρ₀<0，这表明在低温下可能最终恢复"隐藏"的费米液体T²行为。高质量样品的分析对于确认量子临界行为至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在UTe₂中观察到的非费米液体行为可能掩盖了低温下的费米液体行为，需要高质量低剩余电阻率样品来确认或否定被超导掩盖的"隐藏"量子临界行为的存在。

Abstract: The question of a possible quantum critical point lying inside of a superconducting phase is central for understanding unconventional superconductivity. In various unconventional superconductors, non-Fermi-liquid/'strange-metal' $T^{n}$ variations, with $n<2$, of the electrical resistivity have been identified as the signature of magnetic quantum criticality. However, a difficulty is to prove experimentally that a non-Fermi-liquid/'strange-metal' law identified at temperatures above the superconducting temperature is the signature of an intrinsic zero-temperature quantum critical regime. In the heavy-fermion paramagnet UTe$_2$, unconventional superconductivity develops in the vicinity of a metamagnetic quantum phase transition induced by a magnetic field, and the quantum critical magnetic properties are suspected to play a role for the superconducting mechanism. In this work, we present a comparative analysis of electrical resistivity data collected on two UTe$_2$ samples of different qualities, in magnetic fields tilted by angles $θ\simeq35-40$~$^\circ$ from $\mathbf{b}$ to $\mathbf{c}$. Fits to the data have been performed either with a Fermi-liquid function $ρ=ρ_0+AT^{2}$ or with a non-Fermi-liquid/'strange-metal' function $ρ=ρ_0+A_nT^n$. Near to a superconducting phase induced beyond 40~T, non-physical residual resistivities $ρ_0<0$ are extracted from the $T^n$ fits, revealing that a 'hidden' Fermi-liquid $T^2$ regime may be ultimately recovered at low temperature. The results obtained here highlight the importance to investigate high-quality samples with low residual resistivity to confirm - or not - the presence of a suspected 'hidden' quantum critical behavior masked by superconductivity.

</details>


### [122] [Type II Lifshitz invariant and optically active Higgs mode in time-reversal symmetry broken superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15054)
*Raigo Nagashima,Chihiro Mamiya,Naoto Tsuji*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文引入了"第二类"Lifshitz不变量，这是一种在超导体中打破时间反演对称性时出现的粒子-空穴奇对称项，使得希格斯模式在光学电导谱中可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Lifshitz不变量（第一类）是粒子-空穴偶对称的，但超导体中可能存在粒子-空穴奇对称的Lifshitz不变量。研究这种新型不变量对于理解时间反演对称性破缺超导体的光学响应特性具有重要意义，特别是希格斯模式的可观测性。

Method: 1. 引入"第二类"Lifshitz不变量的概念，区别于传统的"第一类"Lifshitz不变量；2. 基于磁点群理论，对所有允许第二类Lifshitz不变量的不可约核心表示对进行分类；3. 对多种时间反演对称性破缺的多带超导体模型进行数值计算，计算光学电导率并与群论分析结果对比。

Result: 1. 证明第二类Lifshitz不变量仅出现在打破时间反演对称性的超导体中；2. 发现第二类Lifshitz不变量使得希格斯模式在光学电导谱中可见；3. 提供了允许第二类Lifshitz不变量的所有不可约核心表示对的完整分类；4. 数值计算结果与群论分析一致，验证了理论的正确性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一类普遍的时间反演对称性破缺超导体，其中希格斯模式具有光学活性。第二类Lifshitz不变量的发现为理解和探测这类超导体的光学响应特性提供了新的理论框架和分类方法。

Abstract: Lifshitz invariant is a symmetry-allowed term in the Ginzburg-Landau free energy of an ordered phase, involving the order parameters and a single spatial derivative, which serves as a source of unusual optical responses. Here we introduce a ``type II" Lifshitz invariant for superconductors, which changes its sign under the particle-hole transformation and can be distinguished from the ordinary particle-hole even ``type I" Lifshitz invariant. We show that the type II Lifshitz invariant appears only in superconductors that break time-reversal symmetry and allows the Higgs mode to be visible in the optical conductivity spectrum. We provide a classification of all pairs of irreducible corepresentations of order parameters in the magnetic point groups that admit a type II Lifshitz invariant. We also numerically calculate the optical conductivity for various models of time-reversal symmetry broken multiband superconductors, finding agreement with the group-theoretical analysis. Our results establish a universal class of time-reversal symmetry broken superconductors hosting an optically active Higgs mode.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum fluctuations and the emergence of in-gap Higgs mode in superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15120)
*Sida Tian,Naoto Tsuji,Dirk Manske*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 量子涨落修正s波超导体中希格斯模的传播子，使其特征频率低于超导能隙2Δ，形成无阻尼极点，显著增强实验信号


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子涨落对s波超导体中希格斯模的影响，解释不同实验技术测量能隙差异的原因，为其他振幅模式系统提供理论洞见

Method: 采用单圈量子修正计算希格斯传播子，分析特征频率偏移，通过计算三次谐波产生和拉曼散射信号验证理论预测

Result: 量子修正使希格斯模特征频率低于2Δ，形成无阻尼极点，导致THG和拉曼散射信号显著增强；解释了不同实验技术测量能隙的差异

Conclusion: 即使弱量子涨落也能使希格斯模频率偏移并产生新极点，这一发现对电荷密度波、反铁磁体、冷原子费米凝聚体等系统的振幅模式研究具有普适意义

Abstract: We extend the well-established action of the Higgs mode in $s$-wave superconductors to include quantum fluctuations (QFs). We find that already one-loop quantum corrections to the Higgs propagator shift its eigenfrequency below the superconducting energy gap $2Δ$. Consequently, the Higgs mode appears as an undamped pole below the quasiparticle continuum, leading to drastically sharper experimental signatures. We demonstrate this by calculating two characteristic fingerprints of the Higgs mode, namely in Third Harmonic Generation (THG) and inelastic Raman scattering signals. More generally, gaps measured in $s$-wave superconductors with different experimental techniques (such as scanning tunneling microscope and Raman scattering) may be different due to fluctuation corrections. Since already arbitrarily weak QFs lead to the shift and to the new pole, our results shed some light on other amplitude modes even for systems with weak QFs, including charge density waves, (anti-) ferromagnets, or cold atom fermionic condensates.

</details>


### [124] [Abrikosov vortices in altermagnetic superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15204)
*A. A. Mazanik,F. S. Bergeret*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: d波交变磁超导体中磁场穿透产生椭圆形涡旋，其取向受交变磁奈尔矢量与磁场相对方向控制，导致磁化曲线非互易性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究交变磁性与超导性共存系统中的磁场穿透行为，特别是交变磁序如何影响超导涡旋结构及其动力学

Method: 理论分析具有共线d波交变磁序的超导体中磁场穿透，推导有效质量各向异性与涡旋形状关系，研究涡旋-涡旋相互作用能随磁场取向的变化

Result: 磁场产生椭圆形涡旋而非圆形阿布里科索夫涡旋，涡旋主轴沿自旋劈裂最大的晶轴；反转磁场平行分量使涡旋重新取向；有效质量各向异性导致磁化曲线非互易性

Conclusion: 交变磁序通过有效质量各向异性控制涡旋形状和取向，为探索交变磁-超导共存系统提供了新的实验途径，适用于本征共存材料和异质结构

Abstract: We study the penetration of an external magnetic field into a superconductor with collinear $d$-wave altermagnetic order. We demonstrate that instead of circular Abrikosov vortices, the magnetic field generates elliptical vortices with their major axis oriented along one of the crystallographic axis, along which the altermagnetic spin splitting is maximal. Upon reversing the component of the magnetic field parallel to the altermagnetic Néel vector, the vortices reorient towards the other crystallographic axis with maximal spin splitting. We demonstrate that this effect originates from an altermagnetism-induced anisotropy of the effective mass, which is controlled by the coupling between the external magnetic field and the Néel vector. As a consequence, a superconducting film hosting such altermagnetic order and containing pinning defects exhibits nonreciprocal magnetization curves under reversal of the magnetic field parallel to its Néel vector, due to the different vortex--vortex interaction energies for the two field orientations. Our results broaden the understanding of the coexistence of altermagnetism and superconductivity, both in materials hosting these orders intrinsically or in superconductor/altermagnet hybrid structures, and open new experimental avenues for exploring supercurrent vortices in these systems.

</details>

### [125] [Coarse Graining Reveals a Fluctuation-theorem-like Asymmetry in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14962)
*Jian Gao,Lufeng Zhang,Ping Fang,Pu Ke,Jin Wu,Yue Liu,Haijun Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于对称性诊断金融市场不可逆性的方法，通过比较多头和空头持仓时间分布的对称性来量化市场波动强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索是否能为金融市场构建类似涨落定理的对称性诊断工具。涨落定理展示了粗粒化如何将微观对称性转化为可观测的不可逆性，作者希望将这一概念应用于金融市场，其中微观层面的每笔交易都包含买卖双方对称性，而交易决策通常基于粗粒化的价格历史。

Method: 方法包括：1) 使用对称的止盈和止损规则；2) 比较从同一价格序列生成的多头和空头交易集合的持仓时间分布；3) 分析两个分布的对数比值；4) 应用Bachelier首次通过基准模型；5) 通过重叠头寸的短时相关性机制解释不对称性。

Result: 在股票指数、个股和加密货币中，两个分布的对数比值显示出稳健的交叉行为：在短持续时间保持近似常数，在尾部呈线性关系，意味着指数型方向不对称性。尾部斜率定义了有效市场温度，作为所选观测尺度上波动强度的操作度量。Bachelier基准模型能捕捉指数尾部但不能解释不对称性，因为多头和空头头寸共享相同的主导衰减率。重叠头寸的短时相关性通过生成方向依赖的次主导松弛谱，为不对称性提供了最小机制。

Conclusion: 这些结果为金融市场建立了一个类似涨落定理的不可逆性诊断工具，更广泛地适用于仅通过粗粒化可观测量可访问的复杂系统。该方法将微观对称性与宏观不可逆性联系起来，为量化市场波动提供了新的操作框架。

Abstract: Fluctuation theorems show how coarse graining transforms microscopic symmetry into observable irreversibility. Here we ask whether an analogous symmetrybased diagnostic can be constructed for financial markets. At the microscopic level, each transaction pairs a buyer and a seller, whereas trading decisions are typically made from coarse-grained price histories. Using symmetric takeprofit and stop-loss rules, we compare the holding-time distributions of long and short trading ensembles generated from the same price series. Across equityindices, individual stocks and cryptocurrencies, the log-ratio of the two distributions shows a robust crossover. It remains nearly constant at short durations but becomes linear in the tail, implying an exponential directional asymmetry. The tail slope defines an effective market temperature, an operational measure of fluctuation intensity on the chosen observation scale. A Bachelier first-passage benchmark captures the exponential tails but not the asymmetry, because long and short positions share the same leading decay rate. By contrast, short-time correlations between overlapping positions provide a minimal mechanism for the asymmetry by generating direction-dependent subleading relaxation spectra in a coarse-grained Markov description. Together, these results establish a fluctuation-theorem-like diagnostic of irreversibility in financial markets and, more broadly, in complex systems accessible only through coarse-grained observables.

</details>


### [126] [Thermodynamic Geometry of Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15000)
*Hao Wang,Li Zhao,Shuai Deng,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出基于瑞利商的几何度量，将弛豫描述为熵刚度和摩擦耗散之间的竞争，以范德瓦尔斯气体为例展示弛豫景观，发现临界温度附近慢模弛豫率线性消失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然平衡态和驱动非平衡过程的几何描述已清楚，但弛豫向平衡的几何描述仍缺乏。本文旨在建立弛豫过程的几何框架，完善热力学几何理论

Method: 提出基于瑞利商的热几何度量，将弛豫重新表述为熵刚度和摩擦耗散之间的基本竞争。以具有两个耗散通道的范德瓦尔斯气体为例，明确展示其弛豫景观

Result: 发现当接近临界温度Tc时，慢模弛豫率线性消失：λ_s ∝ (T-Tc)/Tc，表明临界减慢现象。该几何度量成功描述了弛豫动力学

Conclusion: 本研究完善了热力学几何框架，为表征复杂系统的弛豫动力学提供了一般性工具，建立了弛豫过程的几何描述

Abstract: While the geometry of equilibrium states and driven non-equilibrium processes is clearly understood, a geometric description for relaxation towards equilibrium is still lacking. Here, we propose a thermo-geometric measure based on the Rayleigh quotient, reformulating relaxation as a fundamental competition between entropic stiffness and frictional dissipation. Taking a van der Waals gas with two dissipation channels as an example, we explicitly demonstrate its relaxation landscape. Particularly, we find that upon approaching the critical temperature $T_c$, the slow-mode relaxation rate vanishes linearly as $λ_s \propto (T-T_c)/T_c$, indicating critical slowing down. This study completes the thermodynamic geometry framework, providing a general tool for characterizing the relaxation dynamics of complex systems.

</details>
